However, accurate detection remains challenging due to instance-dependent modality contributions and misleading semantic consistency, where surface-level alignment masks underlying contradictory intent. Existing methods often rely on fixed fusion strategies and treat sarcasm as generic cross-modal mismatch, limiting their ability to capture subtle sarcasm cues and instance-specific modality interactions. To address these challenges, we propose a novel MSD framework that integrates Dynamic Gated Cross-Modal Fusion with Sarcastic-aware Contrastive Regularization (SaCR). Specifically, a bidirectional gated interaction module performs cross-modal feature filtering and adaptively calibrates textual and visual contributions at the instance level. A dynamic fusion gate further balances modality importance to generate more robust multimodal representations. Furthermore, SaCR is introduced as a label-aware contrastive regularization objective that encourages semantic consistency for non-sarcastic samples while suppressing misleading consistency in sarcastic cases. The proposed framework is trained end-to-end with a multi-objective learning strategy that jointly optimizes multimodal classification and auxiliary unimodal supervision. Extensive experiments on MMSD and MMSD2.0 demonstrate that the proposed method consistently outperforms strong baselines.

</details>


### [103] [Natural Language Code Retrieval for 1C:Enterprise: An Open Benchmark and Efficient Bi-Encoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19957)
*Konstantin Chesnokov,Chingiz Mingazov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文为1C:Enterprise生态系统构建了开放代码检索基准（3,413个真实查询-代码对）、可复现评测框架与专用双编码器，通过78万+合成三元组微调、Matryoshka表示学习（MRL）和隐私感知分词器，在nDCG@10上超越基线模型；MRL截断到256维仅损失0.1%检索质量却将索引存储与相似度计算降低三倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 1C:Enterprise将俄语语法与高度领域化的术语相结合，此前几乎没有公开数据集和专用模型用于该领域的自然语言代码检索，因此需要在数据、基准和模型三个层面填补空白。

Method: 提出完整流水线：(1) 构建含3,413个脱敏真实查询-代码对的开放基准及可复现评测工具；(2) 利用google/gemma-4-26B-A4B-it从公开代码库生成784,057条合成三元组以缓解标注稀缺；(3) 训练专用双编码器，采用Matryoshka表示学习（MRL）与隐私感知分词器；(4) 针对子集规模差异，报告均衡子集宏平均、查询加权微平均及仅论坛子集结果；(5) 通过保守的精确/13-gram重叠审计剔除疑似训练-基准重叠样本，检验结果稳健性。

Result: 模型达到0.5992均衡宏平均nDCG@10、0.5044微平均和0.4617论坛子集成绩，优于基线架构的0.4932宏平均与google/embeddinggemma-300m的0.5404；剔除所有被重叠审计标记的样本后仍保持0.6011宏平均（0.5010微平均），说明训练-基准重叠不能解释主要结论；MRL截断至256维保留99.9%的检索质量，同时使稠密索引存储和精确相似度运算是降低三倍。

Conclusion: 该工作为1C代码检索提供了首个开放基准、可复现评测框架和专用双编码器，证明了经合成数据、MRL与隐私感知分词器训练的专用模型能显著超越通用基线，并验证了结果对训练-基准重叠审计的稳健性；MRL降维在几乎不损失质量的前提下大幅降低存储与计算开销。

Abstract: Natural language code retrieval is a rapidly evolving task in computer science. However, the 1C:Enterprise ecosystem combines Russian syntax with highly domain-specific terminology, for which open datasets and specialized models have been virtually non-existent. We present a comprehensive pipeline for 1C code retrieval: an open benchmark of 3,413 real-world, PII-scrubbed query-code pairs, a reproducible evaluation harness, and a specialized bi-encoder. To overcome scarce labeled data, we fine-tune on 784,057 synthetic triplets generated by google/gemma-4-26B-A4B-it from public code repositories, using Matryoshka Representation Learning (MRL) and a privacy-aware tokenizer. Because the benchmark subsets differ in size, we report balanced-subset macro, query-weighted micro, and forum-only results. Our model reaches 0.5992 balanced macro nDCG@10, 0.5044 micro, and 0.4617 on forum, versus 0.4932 macro for the baseline architecture and 0.5404 for google/embeddinggemma-300m. Removing every benchmark example flagged by the conservative exact/13-gram overlap audit leaves 0.6011 balanced macro (0.5010 micro), indicating that detected train-benchmark overlap does not explain the headline result. MRL truncation to 256 dimensions preserves 99.9% of retrieval quality while reducing dense-index storage and exact similarity arithmetic by a factor of three.

</details>


### [104] [Robust Incomplete Multimodal Sentiment Analysis via Iterative Proxy Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19971)
*Zhifa Geng,Subin Huang,Hao Guo,Junjie Chen,Sanmin Liu,Chao Kong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种迭代代理修正框架，用于解决多模态情感分析（MSA）中语言模态缺失或损坏的问题。该框架从非语言模态构建面向语言的代理表示，并通过门控残差修正迭代优化代理，再依据语言可靠性分数与观测到的语言表示自适应融合，同时引入阶段式潜在修正目标作为语义锚点稳定训练，在 MOSI、MOSEI、SIMS 数据集上取得优于基线方法的鲁棒性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实场景下多模态输入常不完整或被破坏，削弱跨模态互补性并引入误导信息；现有一次性代理构建方法生成的代理在初始化时较粗糙、不可靠，过早注入多模态推理会传播初始误差，损害情感预测性能。

Method: 提出迭代代理修正框架：1) 从非语言模态构建面向语言的代理表示；2) 在多模态语境下通过门控残差修正逐步细化代理；3) 依据估计的语言可靠性分数，将修正后代理与观测语言表示自适应融合；4) 引入阶段式潜在修正目标，以完整语言表示为训练时语义锚点稳定代理修正轨迹。

Result: 在 MOSI、MOSEI、SIMS 三个数据集及多种缺失模态设置下的大量实验表明，所提框架一致优于竞争性基线方法，在不完整输入条件下实现鲁棒的情感预测。

Conclusion: 通过迭代代理修正替代一次性代理构建，并配合可靠性引导的自适应融合与阶段式潜在修正目标，有效缓解了不完整多模态输入下的误差传播问题，显著提升情感分析的鲁棒性和准确性。

Abstract: Multimodal sentiment analysis aims to infer affective states by integrating language, visual, and acoustic cues. However, real-world multimodal inputs are often incomplete or corrupted, which can weaken cross-modal complementarity and introduce misleading information into downstream fusion. Existing proxy-based methods for incomplete MSA commonly rely on one-shot proxy construction to compensate for degraded language information, but the generated proxy may be coarse or unreliable at initialization. Prematurely injecting such a proxy into multimodal reasoning can propagate initial errors and compromise sentiment prediction. To address this limitation, we propose an iterative proxy correction framework for robust incomplete MSA. Our method constructs a language-oriented proxy from non-language modalities and progressively refines it under multimodal context through gated residual correction. The corrected proxy is then adaptively fused with the observed language representation according to an estimated language reliability score, allowing the model to balance proxy-based compensation and trustworthy linguistic evidence. In addition, we introduce a stage-wise latent correction objective that uses the complete language representation as a training-time semantic anchor to stabilize the proxy refinement trajectory. Extensive experiments on MOSI, MOSEI, and SIMS under diverse missing-modality settings demonstrate that the proposed framework consistently outperforms competitive baselines and achieves robust sentiment prediction under incomplete inputs.

</details>


### [105] [HealMed: Multilingual Evaluation of Large Language Models in Medicine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19981)
*Yingjian Chen,Fan Gao,Sherry T. Tong,Haoyu Zhang,Aosong Feng,Kevin W. Jin,Xing Wu,Jinghui Lu,Abdul Samad,Akbar Faruqi,Cesar Caraballo,Cibele Brandão,Dhruva,Gupta,Eunji Jeon,Gabriel Madera-Santiago,Geon Lee,Hugo Toshio Itikawa,Insook Cho,Isabelli Martins,Isarar Siddique,Israr Ahmed,Jihyo Kwak,Kanyakorn Veerakanjana,Luis Guilherme Cardoso,Minjin Kim,Piyalitt Ittichaiwong,Renee Dua,Santiago Gudiño-Rosales,Xiujie Chen,Zeo Lapalus,Zixin Xu,Michihiro Yasunaga,Rex Ying,Heuiseok Lim,Jaewoo Kang,Chanjun Park,Hang Jiang,Ethan Goh,Hyunjae Kim,Edison Marrese-Taylor,Yusuke Iwasawa,Yutaka Matsuo,Qingyu Chen,Irene Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HealMed 是一个经专家审校的多语言医学 LLM 评测基准，涵盖 9 种语言、3 种任务格式，发现低资源语言性能下降明显，且翻译质量会实质性影响跨语言评测结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学大语言模型评测大多以英语为主，缺乏高质量、经专家校订的多语言基准，难以可靠衡量模型在跨语言、尤其是低资源语言场景下的真实医学能力。作者旨在构建一个由医学专家参与、翻译经双重审校的基准，以揭示多语言性能差距及其成因。

Method: 构建 HealMed 基准：由 23 位来自 9 个国家和地区的医生与医学专家历时两年开发，包含 9 种语言、每语言 1,000 个样例，源自 9 个数据集，覆盖多选题（MCQA）、自然语言推理（NLI）和开放式问答三种任务格式。每份翻译均由两名精通英语及目标语言的专家评估和修订。随后在多类模型（专有、开源、医学专用）上进行跨语言性能评测。

Result: 在 HealMed 上，低资源语言的性能下降最为明显，但差距大小因语言和模型而异。最强的专有模型跨语言表现最稳定；许多开源模型和医学专用模型则表现出更大且更不稳定的差距。医学专业化本身并不能保证多语言稳健性。此外，专家修订既可能提高也可能降低实测性能，表明翻译质量对跨语言评测结果有实质性影响。

Conclusion: HealMed 为医学 LLM 的多语言评估提供了经专家审校的高质量基准。研究揭示低资源语言性能显著落后，且翻译质量会实质影响评测结果，提示未来跨语言医学 AI 评测需重视翻译质量和专家校订，而不能简单假设医学专业化能带来多语言稳健性。

Abstract: We present HealMed, an expert-reviewed benchmark for multilingual evaluation of large language models in medicine. HealMed contains 1,000 examples in each of nine languages, drawn from nine datasets and covering three task formats: MCQA, NLI and open-ended QA. The benchmark was developed over two years by 23 physicians and medical experts based across nine countries and regions. Each translation was evaluated and revised by two experts fluent in English and the corresponding target language. On HealMed, performance declined most in low-resource languages, although the size of the gap varied markedly across languages and models. The strongest proprietary models were the most stable across languages, whereas many open-source and medically specialized models showed larger and less consistent gaps. Medical specialization alone did not ensure multilingual robustness. Furthermore, expert revision could either raise or lower measured performance, indicating that translation quality materially affects cross-language evaluation results.

</details>


### [106] [Auditing Cross-Lingual Fairness in Language Model Watermarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20047)
*Alexander Nemecek,Osama Zafar,Debargha Ganguly,Vikash Singh,Vipin Chaudhary,Erman Ayday*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文指出，大语言模型输出的水印方案几乎只在英文文本上评估，且仅依赖各方案自身的检测阈值和狭窄的质量指标。多语言部署会暴露这些评估设计选择的局限性。作者提出一个四组件的评估框架，在六种水印、三个开源模型、十一种语言（四种文字、八个类型学语族）上应用后，发现跨语言差异主要来自语族之间的结构性差异，而非个别语言的偶然因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有水印评估几乎完全局限于英文，使用各方案自带的检测阈值和少量质量指标；在多语言场景下，这些在英文上无关紧要的评估设计选择会决定跨语言结论，因此需要一种跨语言、多维度的系统评估框架。

Method: 提出四组件评估框架：(1) 按部署场景经验校准的检测阈值；(2) 与阈值无关的配套测量，用于区分校准失败与检测失败；(3) 三种互不相交的质量测量范式——分布性、配对语义、参考困惑度；(4) 基于类型学语族划分的广义熵分解，用于刻画跨语言差异。该框架应用于六种水印方案、三个开源权重生成器、十一种语言（覆盖四种文字与八个类型学语族），以及基础模型和指令微调两种模式。

Result: 该框架揭示了单语言、单范式评估无法发现的失效模式。在检测与质量两个维度上，观察到的跨语言差异主要发生在类型学语族之间，表明水印的跨语言公平性差距源于语言的结构性属性，而非个别语言的特性。

Conclusion: 跨语言公平性差距是结构性的，与语言类型学属性相关，而非个别语言的偶然现象；多维、阈值无关、类型学分解的评估框架对于正确理解和改进多语言水印方案至关重要。

Abstract: Watermarking schemes for large language model output are evaluated almost exclusively on English text using each scheme's detection threshold and a narrow set of quality measurements. Multilingual deployment exposes evaluation-design choices that are inconsequential on English but determine conclusions cross-lingually. We propose an evaluation framework with four components: detection thresholds calibrated empirically per deployment context, a threshold-independent companion measurement that distinguishes calibration failures from detection failures, three disjoint quality measurement paradigms (distributional, paired-semantic, and reference-perplexity), and a generalized-entropy decomposition of cross-language disparity over a typological family partition. Applied to six watermarking schemes, three open-weight generators, eleven languages spanning four scripts and eight typological families, and both base and instruction-tuned regimes, the framework reveals failure modes that single-language single-paradigm evaluation cannot surface. Across detection and quality, observed disparity is predominantly between-family on the typological partition, indicating that cross-lingual fairness gaps in watermarking are structural to language properties rather than idiosyncratic to particular languages.

</details>


### [107] [SABET-QA: Temporal Knowledge Graph Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20083)
*Brahim Touayouch,Mirette Moawad,Dmitry Akulov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SABET-QA 是一个面向时序知识图谱问答（TKGQA）的框架，通过双向实体-时间评分机制、槽位感知的上下文化模块，以及可微分工作记忆，实现跨多个跳步的推理状态迭代精炼，在多个基准数据集上尤其针对复杂多步时序查询取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时序知识图谱问答需要对时间敏感的事实进行推理，但现有基于嵌入的方法因采用单遍（single-pass）推理流程，难以处理多步（multi-step）查询，无法在多跳之间逐步修正和细化推理状态，导致复杂时序问题上的表现受限。

Method: 提出 SABET-QA 框架，核心包括三部分：（1）双向实体-时间评分机制（bidirectional entity-temporal scoring），支持在实体与时间维度上双向打分；（2）槽位感知的上下文化模块（slot-aware contextualization），将问题语义与时序知识图谱嵌入对齐；（3）可微分工作记忆（differentiable working memory），支持跨多跳渐进式假设精炼；此外，在可用时以辅助时序边界（temporal boundaries）作为粗粒度监督信号。

Result: 在 CronQuestions、Complex-CronQuestions、MultiTQ 和 TimeQuestions 四个基准上取得对强基线的持续改进，尤其在复杂多步时序查询上提升明显。

Conclusion: 通过迭代式多跳推理而非单遍流程，SABET-QA 能有效处理时间敏感的复杂问答，验证了多步状态精炼与时序对齐机制对 TKGQA 的作用。

Abstract: Question Answering over Temporal Knowledge Graphs (TKGQA) requires reasoning over time-sensitive facts, yet existing embedding-based methods struggle with multi-step queries due to single-pass reasoning pipelines. We propose SABET-QA, a framework that iteratively refines reasoning states across multiple hops via a bidirectional entity-temporal scoring mechanism and a slot-aware contextualization module that aligns question semantics with temporal KG embeddings. A differentiable working memory enables progressive hypothesis refinement, while auxiliary temporal boundaries serve as coarse supervision when available. Experiments on CronQuestions, Complex-CronQuestions, MultiTQ, and TimeQuestions demonstrate consistent improvements over strong baselines, particularly on complex multi-step temporal queries.

</details>


### [108] [OenoBench: A Wine-Domain Benchmark for Knowledge-Grounded Evaluation of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20106)
*Nikita Khudov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 OenoBench，一个葡萄与葡萄酒领域知识基准，包含 3,266 道多项选择题，覆盖六大板块和四个难度层级，并通过可溯源、经审计的 LLM 流程构建。对 16 种前沿模型配置的评测揭示了准确率、推理模式提升、自我偏好偏差、开放权重模型成本-准确率前沿以及参数化记忆上限等关键发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 领域知识基准常缺乏严谨的来源可溯性与真实性保障，且对葡萄酒这一兼具专业性与层次性的细分领域覆盖不足。作者旨在构建一个每个论断均可溯源到 URL、经人类金标准校准审计的高质量知识基准，以可靠地测量模型在专业知识上的能力与偏差。

Method: 作者设计了一条 LLM 驱动的数据构建流水线：35 个经来源验证的抓取器从政府注册机构（INAO、TTB、OIV）、同行评审期刊及 Wikipedia/Wikidata 提取 38,104 条原子化、锚定来源的事实；LLM 仅负责改写与审计而非充当事实来源。题目由五大生成器家族的五种策略生成，并经由对照人工金标准（Cohen's κ 校准）的九智能体审计打分，最终构建出 3,266 道多选题，分布于六个板块（产区、葡萄品种、栽培、酿造、生产者、商业）与四个难度层级。

Result: 在 16 种前沿配置上的评测表明：整体准确率介于 53% 至 84%，o3 以 83.6% 领先；推理模式提升集中于 DeepSeek R1（+6.8 个百分点），而在 Claude Opus 与 Gemini Pro 上未见；Anthropic 对自身生成的题目表现出 +9 个百分点的自我偏好，Google 则表现出 -8 个百分点的逆向偏好；前沿开放权重模型与专有推理模型共享成本-准确率帕累托前沿；所有配置在闭卷可解题目上的提升约 33 个百分点，揭示出只有上下文切片可规避的参数化记忆上限。

Conclusion: OenoBench 提供了高质量、可溯源、经审计的葡萄酒领域知识评测工具，揭示了各模型在专业知识的准确性、推理增益与自我偏好上的差异，并量化了参数化记忆的性能上限。语料、审计结果、人工审查应用及构建代码均以 CC-BY-SA-4.0 许可发布。

Abstract: We introduce OenoBench, a wine-domain knowledge benchmark of 3,266 multiple-choice questions across six pillars (regions, grape varieties, viticulture, winemaking, producers, business) and four difficulty tiers. The corpus is built from 38,104 atomic, source-anchored facts extracted by 35 provenance-verified scrapers from government registries (INAO, TTB, OIV), peer-reviewed journals, and Wikipedia/Wikidata. Our methodological contribution is an LLM-driven pipeline in which language models reformat verified facts and audit the result, but never serve as the source of truth: every claim traces to a URL, every question is generated by one of five strategies across five generator families, and every question is scored by a nine-agent audit calibrated against a human gold sheet via Cohen's $κ$. Evaluating sixteen frontier configurations, we find: (i) overall accuracy spans 53%-84%, led by o3 at 83.6%; (ii) reasoning-mode lift concentrates in DeepSeek R1 (+6.8pp) and is absent in Claude Opus and Gemini Pro; (iii) Anthropic shows +9pp self preference on its own questions while Google shows -8pp inverse preference; (iv) frontier open-weight models share the cost-vs-accuracy Pareto frontier with proprietary reasoning models; and (v) every config gains around 33pp on closed-book solvable items, revealing a parametric-recall ceiling that only the contextual slice avoids. We release corpus, audit findings, human-review app, and construction code under CC-BY-SA-4.0.

</details>


### [109] [When Text and Numbers Disagree: Evidence Arbitration in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20116)
*Mattia Carletti,Edward Phillips,Fredrik K. Gustafsson,Patitapaban Palo,Lei Clifton,Danielle Belgrave,Xiao Gu,David A. Clifton*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究大语言模型（LLM）在文本摘要、数值观测与外部工具输出等证据相互冲突时如何进行仲裁决策，并发现其仲裁行为呈现系统性启发式偏好而非随机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 越来越多地被部署在需要整合文本摘要、数值观测和外部工具输出等多源异构证据的决策场景中，但这些证据可能相互矛盾。现有研究缺乏对 LLM 在面对冲突证据时仲裁策略的系统理解，尤其缺乏能精确控制各证据维度（模态、时间新旧、来源可靠性、证据来源）的实验框架。

Method: 作者构建了一个受控的合成基准：由潜在风险轨迹同时生成数值时间序列和自然语言摘要，从而构造出仅有一类证据与真实标签一致的冲突场景。该设计允许独立操纵模态（文本 vs 数值）、时间新近度（temporal recency）、来源可靠性（source reliability）和证据出处（evidence provenance）。在多个开放权重的指令微调模型上评估其仲裁行为。

Result: 研究发现 LLM 的仲裁行为是系统性的而非随机的：模型表现出明确的文本/数值偏好差异；相比显式的可靠性提示，更一致地遵循时间新近度；并且即使外部预测与直接的上下文证据相冲突，模型也可能过度依赖外部预测。

Conclusion: 当前 LLM 在整合异构证据时往往依赖启发式仲裁策略，这暴露了工具增强决策系统的一个失效模式，提示需要改进 LLM 的证据整合与冲突消解能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used in settings where textual summaries, numerical observations, and external tool outputs may provide conflicting evidence. We study how LLMs arbitrate between such sources when they support opposing decisions. To do so, we introduce a controlled synthetic benchmark in which latent risk trajectories generate both numerical time series and natural language summaries, allowing us to construct conflicts where exactly one evidence source is aligned with the ground-truth label. This design lets us independently manipulate modality, temporal recency, source reliability, and evidence provenance. Across open-weight instruction-tuned models, we find that arbitration behaviour is systematic rather than random: models exhibit distinct text-versus-number preferences, follow temporal recency more consistently than explicit reliability cues, and can over-rely on external forecasts even when they conflict with direct contextual evidence. These results suggest that current LLMs often rely on heuristic arbitration strategies when integrating heterogeneous evidence, highlighting a failure mode for tool-augmented decision systems.

</details>


### [110] [FormalTCS: Benchmarking End-to-End Frontier Formal Theoretical Computer Science Research of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20153)
*Dingzirui Wang,Xuanliang Zhang,Keyan Xu,Qingfu Zhu,Wanxiang Che*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个专家验证的基准（含175个来自2025-2026年STOC/FOCS/SODA/COLT录用论文的实例，并附有经专家核验的Lean形式化与证明），用于评估大语言模型在前沿端到端理论计算机科学研究上的能力；研究发现当前模型尚无法可靠完成完整研究流程，其中自动形式化是最大瓶颈，且"研究品味"不足是自主研究的另一主要障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有用于评估LLM理论计算机科学能力的基准与现实研究环境差距较大，缺乏对论文级定义、假设和证明依赖的保留，也难以衡量端到端（从自然语言到形式化再到证明）的真实研究能力，因此需要构建一个更贴近真实TCS研究的前沿基准。

Method: 构建名为ourbenchmark的专家验证基准，包含175个来自2025-2026年STOC、FOCS、SODA、COLT录用论文的实例，保留论文特定的定义、假设和证明依赖，并提供专家验证的Lean形式化和证明；在此基础上对领先LLM进行评估，并进一步开发一个自动化TCS研究框架，该框架能生成、形式化、过滤并证明新命题。

Result: 评估显示当前模型远不能可靠完成完整研究流程：自动形式化是最大瓶颈，最佳模型将自然语言命题翻译为形式化定理陈述的成绩仅为11.5，而对人类提供的形式化陈述进行证明时Pass@8可达28.6。基于该基准开发的框架生成64个新命题，仅6个最终通过专家评估与证明验证。

Conclusion: 当前LLM距离自主完成前沿TCS研究仍有较大差距，自动形式化是首要瓶颈；此外，除形式化能力外，"有限的研究品味"也是阻碍自主TCS研究的关键因素。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown growing potential for automated theoretical computer science (TCS) research, yet existing benchmarks remain far from realistic research settings. We introduce \ourbenchmark, an expert-validated benchmark for evaluating LLMs on frontier, end-to-end TCS research. \ourbenchmark contains $175$ instances drawn from papers accepted to STOC, FOCS, SODA, and COLT in 2025-2026, preserving paper-specific definitions, assumptions, and proof dependencies, with expert-verified Lean formalizations and proofs. Evaluations of leading LLMs reveal that current models remain far from reliably completing the full research pipeline. In particular, autoformalization is the sharpest bottleneck: the best model achieves only $11.5$ on translating natural-language claims into formal theorem statements, compared with $28.6$ Pass@8 when proving human-provided formal statements. Building on \ourbenchmark, we further develop an automated TCS research framework that generates, formalizes, filters, and proves new claims. Of $64$ generated claims, only $6$ ultimately pass expert evaluation and proof verification, indicating that beyond formalization, limited research taste remains another major barrier to autonomous TCS research.

</details>


### [111] [Task-CoEvolve: Efficient Harness Optimization via Adaptive Validation Task Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20169)
*Atsuyuki Miyai,Kiyoharu Aizawa,Toshihiko Yamasaki*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Task-CoEvolve 方法，通过自适应验证任务选择来优化 LLM 智能体 harness，在保持与全量评估相近最终性能的同时，将优化过程中的评估开销降低约 80%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 harness 优化方法在每一轮迭代都完整评估固定的验证集，即使某些任务随着 harness 演化已变得不再具有区分度，仍会产生大量不必要的评估开销。作者希望在不损失最终性能的前提下，降低这一优化过程中的评估成本。

Method: Task-CoEvolve 让验证任务与 harness 共同演化，解决两个关键问题：一是选择有信息量的任务，二是从部分评估估计全量性能。其核心观察是：候选 harness 之间存在分歧的任务比一致成功或一致失败的任务更具区分度。方法采用基于历史结果的方差加权采样，将评估聚焦于智能体能力边界附近的任务，且采样分布随 harness 演化而自适应调整。随后通过对采样概率进行校正，从被采样的任务子集估计全量得分，从而在不同轮次评估不同子集的情况下实现一致比较。

Result: 在在线文本分类和 Terminal-Bench 2.1 上的实验表明，Task-CoEvolve 一致优于固定子集基线，并达到与全量搜索相当的最终性能，同时将优化过程中的评估次数减少约 80%。

Conclusion: 任务选择与 harness 共同演化的策略能够有效降低 LLM 智能体 harness 优化的评估开销，在保持性能的同时显著提升优化效率，代码将开源发布。

Abstract: We present a novel approach to efficient LLM agent harness optimization through adaptive validation task selection. Harness optimization iteratively rewrites the harness code based on validation performance, enabling substantial performance gains without updating the underlying model weights. Existing approaches, however, evaluate a fixed validation set in full at every iteration, incurring substantial evaluation costs even on tasks that become less discriminative as the harness evolves. We propose $\textbf{Task-CoEvolve}$, which co-evolves the validation tasks with the harness by addressing two challenges: selecting informative tasks and estimating full-set performance from partial evaluations. Task-CoEvolve builds on the observation that tasks on which candidate harnesses disagree are more informative for distinguishing among them than tasks that are consistently solved or failed. It uses variance-weighted sampling based on past outcomes to focus evaluation on tasks near the agent's capability frontier, with the sampling distribution adapting as the harness evolves. It then estimates full-set scores from the sampled tasks by accounting for their sampling probabilities, enabling consistent comparisons across iterations despite evaluating different subsets. Experiments on online text classification and Terminal-Bench 2.1 show that Task-CoEvolve consistently outperforms fixed-subset baselines and matches the final performance of full-set search while reducing the number of evaluations during optimization by 80%. Code will be released at https://github.com/Agent4Science-UTokyo/Task-CoEvolve.

</details>


### [112] [Inject, Align, Recover: Staged Post-Training for Retrieval-Free Document Knowledge Internalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20281)
*Qian Kou,Xiaofeng Shi,Xiaosong Qiu,Hua Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 IAR（注入、对齐、恢复）三阶段后训练框架，用于将固定文档语料转化为参数化知识，支持无需检索的文档问答，并在多模型多数据集上取得领域问答与通用能力的双重提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在推理时若未检索到源文档，往往无法回答关于有限文档集合的问题。现有继续预训练等方法在将固定语料内部化时，容易损害模型的通用能力，且难以兼顾领域知识注入与问答行为对齐。

Method: 提出 IAR 三阶段后训练框架：1) Inject（注入）阶段，将源文档转换为续写、重写和指令条件重建三类客观目标，实现结构化知识注入；2) Align（对齐）阶段，仅用“答案式”问答监督微调适配注入后的模型；3) Recover（恢复）阶段，将领域适配模型与基础指令模型合并，以恢复通用能力。

Result: 在 Common Corpus (CC) 与 CCI 数据集，以及 Llama、Phi、Qwen、SmolLM 四个模型家族上，IAR 改进了领域优先-领域通用的前沿。主对比中，IAR 在 8 个数据集-模型设置中的 7 个上，四项指标全部优于 Vanilla SFT，领域 QA 准确率平均提升 3.6 个百分点，IFEval、MMLU、MSBench 的平均通用性能提升 12.1 个百分点。扩展 CC 基线显示，LoRA 和 FAPM 能在单项通用指标上获胜，但在同时达到领先或接近领先领域内部化的方法中，IAR 保持了最强的通用性能之一。

Conclusion: IAR 通过将结构文档知识注入、问答行为对齐与通用能力恢复解耦为三个阶段，有效实现检索无关的文档内部化，在领域问答精度和通用能力上均取得稳健提升，优于 Vanilla SFT 及部分基线方法。

Abstract: Large language models often fail to answer questions about a bounded document collection when the source documents are not retrieved at inference time. We study this setting as document knowledge internalization: converting a fixed corpus into usable parametric knowledge for retrieval-free question answering. We propose IAR (Inject, Align, and Recover), a three-stage post-training framework that separates structured document knowledge injection, QA behavior alignment, and general ability recovery. Unlike conventional continued pretraining, Inject converts source documents into continuation, rewrite, and instruction-conditioned reconstruction objectives. Align then adapts the injected model with answer-only QA supervision, while Recover merges the domain-adapted model with the base instruction model to recover general capabilities. Across Common Corpus (CC) and CCI, and across Llama, Phi, Qwen, and SmolLM model families, IAR improves the domain-primary domain-general frontier for retrieval-free document internalization. In the main comparison, IAR improves over Vanilla SFT on all four reported metrics in 7 of 8 dataset-model settings, with average gains of 3.6 percentage points in domain QA accuracy and 12.1 percentage points in mean general performance across IFEval, MMLU, and MSBench. Extended CC baselines show that LoRA and FAPM can win individual general metrics, but among methods that also reach leading or near-leading domain internalization, IAR retains one of the strongest general profiles.

</details>


### [113] [Inducing Task Models from Computer-Use Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20319)
*Yucheng Jiang,Zora Zhiruo Wang,Ruishi Chen,Diyi Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出任务模型归纳（TMI），能从无约束的计算机使用轨迹中自动发现潜在任务、解交织并发活动，并为每个任务归纳出层次化目标模型与控制流过程模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然化计算机使用轨迹（被动记录的截屏与键鼠操作）是推导符号化、可审计、可复用的日常工作任务模型的宝贵资源。随着计算机使用智能体进入真实工作场景，智能体需要学习任务如何被实际执行，组织也需要审计并复用这些知识。然而，从轨迹中归纳任务模型极具挑战：活动仅以低级事件形式被观察，且真实工作是多线程、目标交织的。现有方法要么假定任务已知，要么只处理单一工作流，且仅产出步骤级摘要而非结构化任务模型。

Method: 提出任务模型归纳（TMI）方法：（1）在无约束轨迹中发现潜在任务，解交织并发活动；（2）对每个潜在任务，归纳一个任务模型，该模型将递归目标分解的层次化目标模型与组织执行的控制流过程模型配对。

Result: 内在评估：在受控人类与智能体轨迹上，TMI 对交织任务的恢复与真实分组的一致度达 0.974，并重建了 74.9% 的观察执行步骤，远超最强的工作流归纳基线。外在评估：由 TMI 任务模型衍生的技能将留出任务的准确率较最强基线提升 30.0%。

Conclusion: TMI 能有效从无约束、交织的计算机使用轨迹中归纳结构化任务模型，其内在重建能力显著优于现有工作流归纳基线，且衍生的技能在实际任务中带来明显准确率提升，验证了该方法在真实工作场景中的潜力。

Abstract: Naturalistic computer-use traces, passively recorded screenshots and mouse or keyboard actions, are a valuable resource for deriving symbolic, auditable, and reusable models of how everyday work is done. Such models matter as computer-use agents enter real work, where agents need to learn how tasks are actually performed, and organizations need to audit and reuse that knowledge. However, inducing such task models is challenging, as activity is observed only as low-level events and real-world work is multi-threaded with interleaved goals. Existing methods assume a given task or a single workflow, and produce step-level summaries rather than structured task models. We introduce Task Model Induction (TMI), which (i) discovers the latent tasks in an unconstrained trace, disentangling concurrent activity, and (ii) for each latent task, induces a task model pairing a hierarchical objective model of recursive goal decomposition with a procedure model of the control flow that organized the execution. Intrinsically, on controlled human and agent trajectories, TMI recovers interleaved tasks with 0.974 agreement against ground-truth groupings and reconstructs 74.9% of the observed execution steps, far more than the strongest workflow induction baseline. Extrinsically, skills derived from TMI's task models improve held-out task accuracy by 30.0% over the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [114] [G-CARL: Grounded Checklist-Aligned Reward Learning for Patient-Oriented Medical Report Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20331)
*Shiao Xie,Siyu Chen,Jianwei Lv,Bo Yuan,Yujin Wang,Xiandong Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出面向患者的医学报告解读任务(PMRI)及G-CARL框架，通过多源检索验证事实、加权清单监督覆盖度，并构建真实基准MMedReport，实验证明其优于现有后训练方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 患者对医学报告的个性化解读需求日益增长，这要求模型同时满足证据支撑的医学事实准确性与上下文相关的患者沟通能力；但现有医学视觉-语言任务未能充分捕捉这一双重需求。此外，事实性与表达性两个目标的可验证性根本不同却紧密耦合，在传统监督微调和整体强化学习范式下难以联合优化。

Method: 作者提出G-CARL——一种基于事实依据、对齐清单的强化学习框架。它结合多源检索进行原子级声明验证，并利用上下文感知、实例特定加权的清单来保证响应覆盖度，从而为事实性、用户需求满足度和表达质量提供结构化监督，而不限制回答的多样性。同时构建了真实世界的PMRI基准MMedReport，并配套临床医生设计的三维评估协议。

Result: 广泛实验表明，G-CARL在整体质量、声明级精确率和清单召回率上均一致优于现有后训练基线。临床医生的成对偏好评估进一步证实，G-CARL生成的解读在准确性和与患者需求的对齐程度上都更优。

Conclusion: G-CARL框架有效解决了PMRI任务中事实准确性与患者个性化沟通难以联合优化的问题，并通过对MMedReport基准的系统评估，证明了其在医学报告个性化解读上的优越性能。

Abstract: Personalized interpretation of medical reports has emerged as an increasingly important need among patients. Addressing this need requires both evidence-grounded medical factuality and context-dependent patient communication, yet existing medical vision-language tasks do not adequately capture these dual requirements. To bridge this gap, we introduce Patient-oriented Medical Report Interpretation (PMRI), a novel open-ended multimodal generation task that requires models to explain medical reports in accurate and accessible language based on a user's query and dialogue history. These two objectives differ fundamentally in their verifiability, yet remain tightly coupled, making them difficult to optimize jointly under conventional supervised fine-tuning and holistic reinforcement learning paradigms. To address this challenge, we propose G-CARL, a grounded, checklist-aligned reinforcement learning framework that combines multi-source retrieval for atomic claim verification with context-aware, instance-specific weighted checklists for response coverage, providing structured supervision for factuality, user-demand satisfaction, and expression quality without constraining response diversity. We further construct MMedReport, a real-world PMRI benchmark, along with a clinician-designed three-dimensional evaluation protocol. Extensive experiments demonstrate that G-CARL consistently outperforms existing post-training baselines in overall quality, claim-level precision, and checklist recall. Pairwise preference evaluation by clinicians further confirms that G-CARL produces interpretations that are more accurate and better aligned with patient needs.

</details>


### [115] [ConceptGuard: Benchmarking Context-Sensitive Unlearning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20338)
*Sahil Kale,Ian Harris*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出概念级遗忘（unlearning）框架，认为现有方法仅针对独立事实的遗忘/保留集合，无法满足消除有害行为同时保留良性知识的需求。作者引入"两用概念"（dual-use concepts），构建基准 ConceptGuard，使遗忘集与保留集在概念使用上显式互补，并以意图敏感的方式评估，以最大化上下文分离。实验表明现有遗忘技术在概念层表现不佳，存在显著的遗忘-效用权衡与概念控制不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 遗忘的方法与基准存在根本缺陷：它们使用由相互独立事实组成的不相交遗忘集与保留集，并通过简单直接的记忆回忆来衡量成功。这种框架无法刻画遗忘的关键要求——既能消除有害行为，又能保留良性、有益的知识。因此，作者主张有效的遗忘必须在概念层面运作，确保彻底移除不安全应用，同时维持概念的正确且有用用法，从而实现概念上有意义且完整的遗忘。

Method: 作者提出"两用概念"（dual-use concepts）——即可同时用于有害与良性语境的概念。在此基础上构建基准 ConceptGuard，使遗忘集与保留集在概念使用上显式互补，从而能在概念层面（而非稀疏事实层面）探索和衡量遗忘。评估采用意图敏感（intent-sensitive）的方式，以最大化上下文分离为目标，促进更安全的行为；并采用 ROUGE 及概念级指标进行评测。

Result: 实验表明，现有遗忘技术在该设定下表现不佳：上下文分离能力弱，ROUGE 与概念级指标性能差。结果揭示了强烈的遗忘-效用权衡、上下文敏感性的提升有限，以及不同方法在概念级控制上缺乏一致性。

Conclusion: 现有 LLM 遗忘方法无法满足现实安全需求，遗漏了概念层面的完整性要求。ConceptGuard 基准为在概念层面探索和评估遗忘提供了新途径。作者据此提出了更能契合现实安全要求的遗忘方法思路，并公开了数据集。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) increasingly require selective removal of harmful or sensitive knowledge, called unlearning, yet existing methods and benchmarks fail to evaluate this capability completely. Current approaches rely on disjoint forget and retain sets composed of independent facts, and measure success using simple and direct factual recall. This framing fails to capture a key requirement of unlearning, namely the ability to eliminate harmful behaviors while preserving benign and beneficial knowledge. We argue that effective unlearning must operate at the level of concepts, ensuring complete removal of unsafe applications while maintaining their correct and useful usage, thereby achieving conceptually meaningful and complete unlearning. To better evaluate unlearning techniques from such a practical viewpoint, we introduce the notion of dual-use concepts: concepts that can be used in both harmful and benign contexts. Building on these concepts, we construct a benchmark called ConceptGuard where forget and retain sets are explicitly complementary in concept usage. Our benchmark uniquely enables unlearning to be explored and gauged at the level of concepts, instead of sparse facts, and evaluation is intent-sensitive with the goal of maximizing contextual separation to promote safer behavior. We demonstrate that current unlearning techniques perform poorly under this setting, showing weak contextual separation alongside poor performance in ROUGE and concept-level metrics. Our results reveal strong forgetting-utility trade-offs, limited gains in contextual sensitivity, and poor consistency in concept-level control across methods, and provide ideas for unlearning approaches that better align with real-world safety requirements. Our dataset is publicly available.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [116] [Improved Confidence Estimates for Black-Box Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19323)
*Sokhna Diarra Mbacke,Mouloud Belbahri,Gabriel Loaiza-Ganem*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用已有标注数据集来提升 LLM 不确定性量化（UQ）的方法：以现有零样本 UQ 分数和相似查询的正确性为特征，训练简单分类器预测 LLM 回答是否正确，从而以极小计算开销持续优于现有分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 的安全部署需要不确定性量化，但现有方法（如口头置信度、多重生成等）通常是零样本的，在部署前总需在目标数据集上评估其性能。作者认为这一现成评估数据未被充分利用，可借此进一步提升 UQ 表现。

Method: 将零样本得到的各类 UQ 分数与"相似查询的正确性"作为特征，训练简单分类器来预测 LLM 回答是否正确。该方法复用部署前已有的标注数据集，计算开销极小。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在产品化场景下持续优于现有的零样本 UQ 分数，且几乎不增加计算负担，是一种廉价且直接的增强方案。

Conclusion: 利用部署前必然存在的评估数据集，以轻量分类器融合现有 UQ 分数与相似查询正确性，能在极低开销下稳定提升 LLM 不确定性量化的实际表现。

Abstract: Uncertainty quantification (UQ) is essential for the safe deployment of large language models (LLMs). Existing methods, from verbalized confidence to ones requiring multiple generations, are often zero-shot and produce scores quantifying uncertainty without the need for labelled data. Nonetheless, in practice one must always evaluate their performance on a dataset of interest before deployment. In this work we show that, by leveraging this dataset, we consistently outperform these existing scores. Specifically, we build simple classifiers that predict LLM response correctness by using these scores and the correctness of similar queries as features. Our method produces minimal computational overhead, making it a cheap and straightforward enhancement for UQ in LLMs for real-world applications.

</details>


### [117] [Towards On-Board Implementation of ML-Based Helicopter Weight Estimator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19210)
*Nicolas Valot,Ammar Mechouche,Benjamin Lesage,Claire Pagetti,Louis Fabre*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于监督式机器学习（LSTM 循环神经网络）的直升机起飞重量估算模型，并依据 EASA 概念文件与 EUROCAE ED-324 开展学习保障（Learning Assurance），在航电老平台上验证其可部署性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直升机在起飞阶段的重量是安全关键参数，直接影响告警等关键功能的准确性。传统估算方法精度或适应性有限，作者希望借助大规模服役机队数据训练机器学习模型，同时满足适航与标准组织对机载 ML 应用的可信性、可保障性要求，使模型能够部署于关键功能。

Method: 利用空客全球在役直升机机队的大规模数据集，训练一个监督式 LSTM 循环神经网络，用于起飞阶段重量估算；并依据 EASA 机器学习应用概念文件与 EUROCA ED-324，建立学习保障流程，提出一套机器学习需求（ML Requirements）、编写机器学习模型描述（ML Model Description），随后在实现上对该需求进行验证，并在传统/遗留航电计算机上进行演示。

Result: 所提 LSTM 重量估算器在遗留航电计算机上成功实现，验证了机器学习需求得到满足，表明该实现适合将机器学习重量估算模型部署到机载目标平台，用于机上告警等关键功能。

Conclusion: 通过将学习保障流程（EASA 概念文件与 EUROCA ED-324）具体化为机器学习需求、模型描述及其验证，证明机器学习模型（LSTM）可在资源受限的遗留航电平台上实现，并具备服务于安全关键机载功能（如起飞阶段机上告警）的部署潜力。

Abstract: This paper focuses on the implementation of a novel supervised Machine Learning model for estimating helicopter weight during takeoff, utilizing extensive datasets from Airbus's global in-service fleet. The study details a learning assurance process aligned with the EASA concept paper for machine learning application, and with the on-going Eurocae ED-324. We propose a set of Machine Learning Requirements, a Machine Learning Model Description, and its implementation for a long short-term memory recurrent neural network. Finally, we verify the requirements on the implementation. Demonstrated on legacy avionics computers, the implementation is suitable for the deployment of the developed Machine Learning Model weight estimator on airborne targets for critical functions such as on-board alerting.

</details>


### [118] [DeltaMomentum: A Key-Value based Anisotropic Momentum Update via Delta Rule](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19491)
*Euijin Hong,Guannan Qu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 DeltaMomentum，一种利用线性层梯度中键-值结构、通过 delta 规则更新动量缓冲区的方向感知型动量方法，能以可忽略的额外开销显著加速各类优化器的收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代优化器通常将动量构造为过去梯度的指数移动平均（EMA），对所有方向以同一固定速率遗忘；但深度网络训练中遇到的输入高度各向异性，少数方向被频繁查询而多数方向很少出现。现有方法仅在缓冲外做额外处理，动量更新本身并未改变。作者希望将方向感知直接融入动量更新规则中。

Method: 核心观察是线性层的梯度可拆解为"输入作为键、输出侧误差作为值"的结构。据此，DeltaMomentum 利用典型 delta 规则更新动量缓冲区，使每个方向的遗忘速率由其出现频率决定。文中证明了它是一个有效动量、能在无矩阵求逆下施加输入侧曲率校正，并在固定与漂移最优解下都比 EMA 更快清除过时方向。它可作为任意优化器动量缓冲区的直接替代，其系数在 μP 下可跨宽度迁移，额外计算为 gated-MLP 块线性成本的 22.2%~25.0%，且无持久内存开销。

Result: 在 FineWeb-Edu 预训练中，使用 DeltaMomentum 的 AdamW（DeltaAdamW）在 67M 规模上最多以少 46.39±4.32% 的步数、在 370M 规模上以少 22.12±0.80% 的步数（三个种子）达到 AdamW 的验证损失，且该增益在 1B 规模 Chinchilla 最优预算下持续存在。在同协议下微调的 Muon 基线在两个语言模型规模上仍高于 DeltaAdamW；该增益对 SGD、ResNet-18 和 CIFAR-10 上的 ViT-Tiny 同样成立。训练期诊断证实了预测机制，即更好的梯度跟踪和更健康的输入方向。

Conclusion: DeltaMomentum 通过将方向感知直接内建于动量更新规则，以低成本显著提升收敛速度，在多种模型、优化器和规模上表现稳健，验证了按输入方向出现频率差异化遗忘的有效性。

Abstract: Most modern optimizers form their momentum as an exponential moving average (EMA) of past gradients, forgetting every direction at one fixed rate. However, the inputs a deep network sees during training can be highly anisotropic, with a few directions queried frequently while most are seen rarely. Recent methods address this anisotropy by wrapping extra processing around this buffer, leaving the momentum update itself unchanged. We propose DeltaMomentum, which builds direction-awareness into the momentum update rule. The main observation is that the gradient of a linear layer splits into an input that acts as a key and an output-side error that acts as a value. Exploiting the key-value structure, DeltaMomentum updates the momentum buffer by the canonical delta rule, so each direction is forgotten at a rate set by how often it appears. We prove that it is a valid momentum, that it applies the input-side curvature correction without matrix inversion, and that it clears stale directions faster than EMA under both a fixed and a drifting optimum. It is a drop-in replacement for the momentum buffer of any optimizer, its coefficient transfers across widths under $μ$P, and its extra compute stays between $22.2\%$ and $25.0\%$ of a gated-MLP block's linear cost with no persistent memory. In FineWeb-Edu pretraining, AdamW with DeltaMomentum (DeltaAdamW) reaches AdamW's validation loss in up to $46.39 \pm 4.32\%$ fewer steps at 67M and $22.12 \pm 0.80\%$ at 370M over three seeds, and the gain persists at 1B on a Chinchilla-optimal budget. A Muon baseline tuned under the same protocol sits above DeltaAdamW at both language-model scales, and the gain holds for SGD, ResNet-18, and ViT-Tiny on CIFAR-10. Training-time diagnostics confirm the predicted mechanism, better gradient tracking and healthier input directions.

</details>


### [119] [Triangular Fuzzy Rescaling Distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19234)
*Eddy Soria,Aida Valls,Ana Beatriz Hernández-Lara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种三角模糊重标度距离 d_{TR}，将线性重标度直接融入距离计算，实现对异构模糊数据的一体化归一化与距离度量，并证明其满足度量性质及有界性、尺度不变性与原点不变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多模糊方法中的距离度量假设所有值处于相同尺度，应用于具有不同尺度或单位的异构属性时需要预先归一化。作者旨在设计一种无需单独预处理阶段、在比较三角模糊数过程中自动完成归一化的距离度量，以解决异构数据带来的尺度不一致问题。

Method: 提出三角模糊重标度距离（d_{TR}），将线性重标度（LRE）直接集成到距离计算公式中。作者形式化证明了 d_{TR} 满足非负性、同一性、对称性和三角不等式等度量性质，并进一步论证其具有有界性、尺度不变性和原点不变性，同时引入加权向量以支持维度优先级。

Result: d_{TR} 被证明是一个真正的度量（满足非负性、同一性、对称性与三角不等式），并具备有界性、尺度不变性和原点不变性。结合加权向量，d_{TR} 适用于异构模糊数据场景。

Conclusion: d_{TR} 通过将归一化融入距离计算，有效解决了异构模糊属性的距离度量问题，适用于构造合成指标、基于距离的机器学习算法以及多准则决策辅助等应用。

Abstract: Decision-making in complex systems often involves dealing with imprecise or uncertain information, frequently represented using fuzzy sets, particularly Triangular Fuzzy Numbers (TFNs). A crucial aspect of many fuzzy methods is the quantification of distance between TFNs. Many distance measures assume that all values are in the same scale, requiring a preliminary normalization stage when applied to heterogeneous attributes with different scales or units. This paper proposes the Triangular Fuzzy Rescaling Distance (d_{TR}), a metric designed to address this challenge. The d_{TR} uniquely integrates Linear Rescaling (LRE) directly into the distance calculation, ensuring normalization during the comparison of fuzzy numbers. We formally prove that d_{TR} satisfies the properties of a metric, including non-negativity, identity, symmetry, and the triangle inequality. Furthermore, we demonstrate that d_{TR} is bounded, scale-invariant, and origin-invariant. These properties, combined with a weighting vector for prioritizing dimensions, make d_{TR} suitable for applications involving heterogeneous fuzzy data, such as the construction of synthetic indicators, distance-based machine learning algorithms or multicriteria-decision aiding.

</details>


### [120] [Holtercare-Bench: A Multimodal Benchmark for Evaluating Long-Term Dynamic ECG Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19297)
*Yihan Xie,Hanwen Cui,Runze Ye,Juekai Lin,Haoyang Wang,Jinhao Mao,Bo Zhang,Wenqiao Zhang,Xiaogang Guo,Jun Xiao,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Holtercare-23K（一个含22,980个QA对、基于788份临床Holter记录的多模态动态心电数据集）和Holtercare-Bench（评估时序定位、临床诊断、全局摘要的多模态基准），并发现主流多模态大语言模型在超长病理序列上存在显著性能缺口，但微调可带来大幅提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在医疗应用中偏向静态图像或短期信号，而在动态心电图（ECG）这一关键领域，由于缺乏高质量数据集和基准，模型难以进行复杂时序推理和诊断报告生成。

Method: 构造Holtercare-23K大规模多模态动态心电数据集（788份临床Holter记录生成22,980个QA对），采用新颖的信号-视频-文本三模态对齐；并基于该数据集构建Holtercare-Bench基准，从时序定位、临床诊断和全局摘要三个维度评估模型；对主流MLLMs进行零样本评估，并对代表性模型进行微调。

Result: 零样本评估显示主流MLLMs在处理超长病理序列上存在显著性能差距；对代表性模型进行微调后取得了显著性能提升。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了当前MLLMs在心电生理领域的局限性，并为长期医疗多模态大语言模型提供了基础性基准。项目代码开源，地址为 https://github.com/ZJU4HealthCare/Holtercare-Bench。

Abstract: While multimodal large language models (MLLMs) excel in medical applications, most of them favor static images or short-term signals. In the critical field of dynamic electrocardiograms (ECG), models struggle with complex temporal reasoning and diagnostic report generation due to a lack of high-quality datasets and benchmarks. To address this, we introduce (i) Holtercare-23K, a large-scale multimodal dynamic ECG dataset comprising 22,980 QA pairs derived from 788 clinical Holter records and featuring a novel signal-video-text tri-modal alignment. Based on this dataset, we present (ii) Holtercare-Bench, a multimodal benchmark that evaluates models on temporal localization, clinical diagnosis, and global summarization. Zero-shot evaluations of leading MLLMs reveal a significant performance gap in processing ultra-long pathological sequences. However, fine-tuning representative models yields substantial improvements. This work illuminates the limitations of current MLLMs in electrophysiology and provides a foundational benchmark for long-term medical MLLMs. Our project is available at https://github.com/ZJU4HealthCare/Holtercare-Bench.

</details>


### [121] [Kähler landscapes for complex neural network descents and guarantees including a search and destroy of the Calabi-Yau manifold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19584)
*Andrew Gracyk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究复参数神经网络的损失景观，通过信息几何与复几何（Kähler/Wirtinger Hessian）描述自然梯度下降路径，并重点分析 Calabi-Yau 信息流形上由病态曲率（负截面曲率、负定 Ricci 曲率、近似低秩度量）导致的优化退化与失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以信息论流形视角刻画复参数神经网络，并借助经典优化保证（经 Dolbeault 渐近等复几何工具）理解其景观结构；动机在于揭示复杂几何约束下优化保证失效的机制。

Method: 在交叉熵下，通过 log-likelihood 势上的 Wirtinger Hessian 构造 Kähler 信息度量；采用自然梯度下降（损失微分乘以度量逆）使下降路径保持在全纯切丛中。重点研究非紧致设定下由全局势定义的 Calabi-Yau 度量：楔积的处处非零全纯形式等于 Kähler 形式顶外积（至常数），得到常数行列式条件。在固定行列式下，证明度量近似低秩（至特征值容差）会导致 blow-up 效应；并讨论负截面曲率与负定 Ricci 曲率对景观的破坏及其关联，同时结合初始化时的渐近与神经网络失败模式建立与深度学习理论的联系。

Result: 在 Calabi-Yau 信息流形上，常数行列式条件下，近似低秩度量导致 blow-up 效应；负截面曲率会破坏损失景观，并与负定 Ricci 曲率相互关联，导致优化保证的失效模式。

Conclusion: 复参数网络景观可通过几何分析模态刻画：Kähler 信息度量下的自然梯度下降保持在全纯切丛，但 Calabi-Yau 流形的病态曲率条件（负曲率、近似低秩、blow-up）会破坏理论保证，说明复杂几何多样性下经典优化保证的局限性。

Abstract: We study landscapes for complex-parameterized networks. Our approach is motivated with an information-theoretic manifold perspective of the parameter and via classical optimization guarantees although of complex geometric variety such as through Dolbeault asymptotics. The descent path admits a Kähler information metric under a cross-entropy via the Wirtinger Hessian on the log-likelihood potential. We restrict attention to a descent update rule with natural gradient descent via a differentiated loss scaled by the inverse metric, so the descent path remains in the holomorphic tangent bundle. We emphasize Calabi-Yau information manifolds which profane theoretical guarantees via an ill-curvature-conditioned landscape. Under a Calabi-Yau metric, specifically in a non-compact setting with a global potential so defined geometrically rather than invoking the topological requirements of the Calabi conjecture, a wedged nowhere-vanishing holomorphic form is the top exterior product of the Kähler form up to constants, yielding a constant determinant condition. Under a fixed determinant, a metric almost low rank up to an eigenvalue tolerance implies a blow-up effect. Moreover, it has been discovered that negative curvature subverts the loss landscape, specifically sectional curvature, so we expand on this and draw interconnections to negative-definite Ricci curvature. Our arguments primarily exist in a geometric analytic modality, although we establish roots in deep learning theory such as through asymptotics at initialization and connections through failure modes of neural network guarantees under vanishing and negative Ricci curvature.

</details>


### [122] [Quantum Kernel Estimation for the Discovery of Early Lung Cancer Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19304)
*Hamed Javidi,Alex Zajichek,Hakan Doga,Laxmi Parida,Filippo Utro,Peter J. Mazzone*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究评估了量子-经典混合机器学习方法（基于保真度的量子核，结合角度/稠密角度特征映射与多种纠缠策略）在基于cfDNA片段组学和DNA甲基化的肺癌检测中的表现，并与经典SVM基线对比，发现量子核模型在部分配置下具备竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低剂量CT肺癌筛查虽能降低死亡率，但受限于接受度、依从性和管理挑战；基于血液cfDNA的生物标志物可作补充，然而肺癌异质性及高维非线性分子信号使早期检测困难，因此探索量子核方法捕获非线性结构的能力。

Method: 经特征选择后，使用20维和40维特征子集训练模型；采用角度（angle）和稠密角度（dense-angle）特征映射及多种纠缠策略将特征编码进量子希尔伯特空间；用精确的statevector模拟计算保真度量子核，并与预计算核SVM和核PCA逻辑回归集成，同在原特征上训练的SVM基线对比。

Result: 重复的留出法评估显示量子核模型在两个数据集上均具竞争力。片段组学中多个20特征配置的AUC优于经典SVM基线，说明其有效捕获了cfDNA片段化非线性结构；甲基化中经典SVM取得最高AUC，但部分量子模型仍具竞争力且在某些情况下特异性更高。特征从20增至40未持续提升性能，反而常增大变异性。

Conclusion: 量子核方法是基于cfDNA的肺癌检测中一种有前景的方法，其编码与纠缠设计对分类效果的影响得到了系统性评估。

Abstract: Lung cancer screening with low-dose chest computed tomography reduces mortality, but its impact is limited by uptake, adherence, and management challenges. Blood-based cell-free DNA (cfDNA) biomarkers offer a complementary approach, although early detection remains difficult because of lung cancer heterogeneity and high-dimensional, nonlinear molecular signals. We evaluated quantum-classical hybrid machine learning for lung cancer detection using DNA fragmentomics and DNA methylation. After feature selection, models were trained using 20- and 40-feature subsets. Features were encoded into quantum Hilbert space using angle and dense-angle feature maps with multiple entanglement strategies. Fidelity-based quantum kernels were computed with exact statevector simulation and integrated with precomputed-kernel SVM and kernel-PCA logistic regression and compared with an SVM model trained on the original features.
  This framework enabled systematic evaluation of how encoding and entanglement design affect classification. Across repeated held-out evaluations, quantum-kernel models achieved competitive performance on both datasets. For fragmentomics, several 20-feature configurations improved AUC relative to a classical SVM baseline, suggesting effective capture of nonlinear cfDNA fragmentation structure. For methylation, the classical SVM achieved the highest AUC, although selected quantum models remained competitive and improved specificity in some cases. Increasing features from 20 to 40 did not consistently improve performance and often increased variability. Overall, these results support quantum kernel methods as a promising approach for cfDNA-based lung cancer detection.

</details>


### [123] [Time-Uniform Self-Normalized Concentration for Discounted Least Squares: Limits and Corrections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19643)
*Yi-Shan Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出广泛使用的加权(折现)自规范化浓度不等式所声称的时间一致(time-uniform)保证并不成立，并通过最小反例、下界构造与错误定位给出了严格论证，同时提供了有效的固定时间版本与修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非平稳问题中，折现最小二乘估计器的加权自规范化浓度不等式常被当作标准工具，并被扩展为声称具有时间一致(anytime)保证。但作者怀疑这一加权扩展的时间一致版本存在缺陷，需要严格检验其正确性。

Method: 首先通过一个参数固定的标量高斯反例，证明声称的有界半径以概率1被穿越。其次，对于固定的折现与正则化参数，在δ≤1/2且T/δ充分大的条件下，证明任何在条件次高斯模型类上普遍有效、确定性的anytime边界，必然在某个时刻达到R√log(T/δ)量级(对非递减边界，该量级在时刻T出现)。最后定位证明错误：不同终止时间使用不同的高斯混合分布，故固定时间混合不构成单一上鞅，且停时论证无法修复这一失效。

Result: 原加权不等式的时间一致保证被证伪：声称的半径在概率1意义下必然被穿越；并给出了T时刻前某处(或非递减边界下在T时刻)不可低于R√log(T/δ)量级的下界。作者同时证明原加权不等式在每个固定确定性时刻仍然成立，并给出有限时域与无限时域的有效修正。

Conclusion: 被广泛使用的折现自规范化浓度不等式的时间一致扩展是错误的，其在anytime意义下不成立；论文定位了混合分布不构成单一上鞅这一核心证明缺陷，并给出固定时间版本及有限/无限时域的修正方案，提示下游分析(如bandit与强化学习)需相应调整。

Abstract: Self-normalized concentration inequalities are standard tools in bandit and reinforcement-learning analyses. A widely used weighted extension claims an analogous time-uniform guarantee for discounted least-squares estimators in non-stationary problems. A simple scalar Gaussian counterexample with a fixed parameter shows that the claimed bounded radius is crossed with probability one. For fixed discount and regularization parameters, we further show that, when $δ\leq1/2$ and $T/δ$ is sufficiently large, any deterministic anytime boundary valid uniformly over the stated conditionally sub-Gaussian model class must be at least of order $R\sqrt{\log(T/δ)}$ at some time by horizon $T$; for nondecreasing boundaries, this order is required at time $T$. We identify the proof error: different terminal times use different Gaussian mixing distributions, so the fixed-time mixtures do not form one supermartingale, and the stopping-time argument does not repair this failure. Finally, we show that the weighted inequality remains valid at each fixed deterministic time, give valid finite- and infinite-horizon corrections, and discuss consequences for downstream analyses.

</details>


### [124] [Finite-Horizon Input-Output Dynamics of Minibatch Perturbations in AdamW](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19762)
*Kang Liu,Suyan Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过将AdamW建模为有限时域的输入-状态-输出（ISO）系统，研究单个minibatch因优化器状态记忆而对未来训练产生的延迟影响，并推导出映射梯度扰动到未来损失效应的符号响应算子及相关误差分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于AdamW在其优化器状态中存储历史梯度信息，单个minibatch的影响会超越其被观测时的那次参数更新。作者希望量化并理解这种延迟效应的幅度、时机与符号，以揭示优化器记忆在训练动力学中的作用。

Method: 作者采用仅在一个梯度更新上存在差异、随后共享同一训练序列的成对轨迹进行研究；将AdamW形式化为有限时域输入-状态-输出（ISO）系统，其状态包含模型参数与一阶、二阶矩估计；对联合动力学进行线性化，得到有符号的响应算子，将局部梯度扰动映射到未来的损失效应；进一步导出精确的多步误差分解，并在局部光滑与受控激活切换条件下建立一阶有限时域精度。

Result: 实验验证了所提出的响应机制及优化器状态效应；重复未来分析（repeated-future analyses）表明延迟影响存在显著的预期式（prospective）结构，且该结构可部分通过ISO近似恢复。

Conclusion: 该工作为理解和刻画优化器记忆导致的延迟训练影响提供了一套可分析的ISO框架与响应算子，揭示了延迟影响的幅度、时机与符号规律，并展示了其近似可恢复的前瞻性结构。

Abstract: A minibatch can influence training beyond the update at which it is observed because AdamW stores past gradient information in its optimizer states. We study this delayed effect through paired trajectories that differ only in one gradient update and share the same subsequent training sequence. We formulate AdamW as a finite-horizon input--state--output (ISO) system whose state contains the model parameters and first- and second-moment estimates. Linearizing the joint dynamics yields a signed response operator that maps a localized gradient perturbation to its future loss effects, revealing how optimizer memory shapes their magnitude, timing, and sign. We further derive an exact multistep error decomposition and establish first-order finite-horizon accuracy under local smoothness and controlled activation switching. Experiments validate the response mechanism and optimizer-state effects, while repeated-future analyses reveal substantial prospective structure in delayed influence that can be partially recovered from ISO approximations. Code is available at https://github.com/Kanyooo/Loss_ISO.

</details>


### [125] [Mechanistic Tomography: Designed Measurement for Control-Oriented Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19338)
*Vijay Erramilli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"机制层析成像"（mechanistic tomography）的统一框架，将各类机制可解释性测量（patching、梯度、Hessian-向量积、子集干预等）抽象为 y = Ax + w 的测量模型，并给出从低成本测量开始、用留出干预验证、按需扩张测量族的实用流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机制可解释性需要推断模型未直接暴露的信息（表征状态、组件效应、交互、干预响应），但不同方法在不同访问条件下测量不同目标量，缺乏统一的理论语言和系统化的验证流程，难以判断测量是否充分、何时需要更复杂的测量族。

Method: 将测量统一建模为 y = Ax + w：A 描述干预、x 为目标映射、w 包含非线性响应、采样误差和基失配。提出实用流程：先用最低成本测量，在预期尺度上用留出干预检验，校准简单失配，当结构化残差存在时扩张测量族。在双 HMM、梯度访问（有限探针）、提升测量与 Hessian-向量积、Tracr 及 GPT-2-small IOI、Qwen-2.5-7B 等设置上进行实验验证。

Result: 控制任务中控制误差随观察者误差上升，而目标改进可能掩盖 nuisance-state 移动；forward-only 访问下稀疏聚合测量用比坐标 patching 更少的干预恢复有限效应映射；梯度访问下有限探针改善局部归因映射；提升测量和 Hessian-向量积能恢复一阶映射遗漏的交互；GPT-2-small IOI 上 Name Mover-Negative Name Mover 交互是三个被测跨组对中最大的留出预测项；Qwen-2.5-7B 上有限校准使可加拒绝响应映射已足够，留出误差不支持成对提升。

Conclusion: 机制层析成像为机制可解释性提供了统一的测量结构语言和系统化验证程序，强调测量充分性必须通过留出干预在预期尺度上检验；测量族的选择依赖于基，简单的可加模型在适当校准后可能已足够，而复杂交互需要提升测量与高阶量才能恢复。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability seeks quantities that models do not expose directly: represented states, component effects, interactions, and responses to interventions. Patching, gradients, Hessian-vector products, and subset interventions provide different measurements under different access assumptions and may target different quantities. We formulate their shared measurement structure as mechanistic tomography: designed measurement for recovering internal mechanisms and intervention effects.
  For a chosen basis and intervention family, measurements take the form y = Ax + w, where A describes the interventions, x is the target map, and w contains nonlinear response, sampling error, and basis misspecification. This language gives a practical procedure: start with the least costly measurements, test on held-out interventions at the intended scale, calibrate simple mismatch, and expand the measurement family when structured residuals remain.
  Control provides a demanding validation setting because an estimate that guides an intervention acts as an observer. In a two-HMM model, control error rises with observer error, while target improvement can hide nuisance-state movement. Under forward-only access, sparse aggregate measurements recover a finite-effect map with fewer interventions than coordinate patching. With gradient access, finite probes improve a local attribution map. Lifted measurements and Hessian-vector products recover interactions missed by first-order maps, while Tracr shows that the required family depends on the basis. On GPT-2-small IOI, the Name Mover-Negative Name Mover interaction is the largest held-out predictive term among three tested cross-group pairs. On Qwen-2.5-7B, finite calibration makes an additive refusal-response map adequate, so held-out error does not support pairwise lifting.

</details>


### [126] [Uncovering the Limits of Proof Sharing for Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19351)
*Kanak Das,Shubham Ugare,Bor-Yuh Evan Chang,Sasa Misailovic,Gagandeep Singh,Manu Sridharan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了神经网络鲁棒性验证中基于模板的证明复用加速技术的有效性与局限，提出"联合稳定神经元"指标解释加速效果差异，并设计 FastCert 方法自动分配模板，在 L0 验证任务上平均提速 1.13 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络鲁棒性验证在多个关键领域日益重要，基于模板的证明共享虽被证明能在某些场景加速不完备验证，但模板复用效果是否随网络架构、性质、数据集和训练方法的不同而稳健，尚不明确。

Method: 本文对基于模板加速的有效性进行系统实证研究，提出"联合稳定神经元"这一新指标以解释模板包含率的差异；并设计 FastCert 技术，自动在网络各层间分配模板，当模板难以带来加速时则完全弃用。

Result: 研究表明模板包含率在不同场景下差异巨大；"联合稳定神经元"指标能解释这种差异，并显示某些情况下模板技术几乎不可能带来提速。在大量基于覆盖设计的 L0 验证任务上，FastCert 相较现有模板复用技术平均提速 1.13 倍。

Conclusion: 模板复用加速并非在所有场景都稳健有效，其效果受网络与任务特征制约；通过"联合稳定神经元"指标可预判加速可行性，FastCert 通过自适应模板分配实现了稳健且略有提升的加速性能。

Abstract: Robustness verification of neural networks is increasingly important, due to their use in many critical domains. In certain scenarios, proof sharing has been shown to accelerate incomplete verification techniques by reusing intermediate-layer abstract states, or templates, across queries. However, questions remain as to the robustness of template-based acceleration across varying network architectures, properties, datasets, and training methods. In this work, we perform a systematic study of the effectiveness of template-based acceleration and its limits. Our study shows that template subsumption rates can vary widely across scenarios. We present a novel metric of jointly stable neurons to explain this variation, showing that in some cases template-based techniques are very unlikely to provide any speedup. Then, we present FastCert, a novel technique for automatically distributing templates across neural network layers to increase performance impact, eschewing templates entirely if they are unlikely to produce a speedup. Across a large set of covering-design based $L_0$-verification tasks, FastCert achieved an average speedup of 1.13x over an extant template-based reuse technique.

</details>


### [127] [Longitudinal Bayesian Learning of Continuous Disease Position across the Alzheimer's Disease Continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19436)
*Yingying Zhang,Kun Zhao,Guodong Liu,Qi Huang,Pengfei Gu,Dongchul Kim,Erik Enriquez,Alex D. Leow,Paul M. Thompson,Heng Huang,Hongchang Gao,Liang Zhan,Haoteng Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出疾病连续体定位（DCP）框架，利用纵向DTI数据和弱临床监督，以低维概率潜变量连续估计阿尔茨海默病严重程度，并派生带不确定性的疾病连续体评分（DCS），在ADNI上验证其优于现有方法且具有临床相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿尔茨海默病是连续的生物学进展过程，但现有基于神经影像的人工智能方法大多局限于横断面影像的离散诊断或临床评分预测，无法连续刻画疾病严重程度，因此需要一种能够从纵向影像中连续定位疾病位置的方法。

Method: 提出疾病连续体定位（DCP），一种纵向贝叶斯学习框架。DCP通过联合整合纵向观测与弱临床监督，将疾病严重程度建模为低维概率潜变量，并由此派生疾病连续体评分（DCS），用于量化个体在AD连续体上的位置及其不确定性。

Result: 在ADNI队列上的大量实验表明，DCP持续优于代表性的疾病进展方法。验证分析显示DCS能准确表征疾病严重程度，具有较强临床相关性，保留纵向疾病演化信息，并能预测未来疾病转化。

Conclusion: DCS提供了一种定量的影像衍生表征，可超越传统诊断标签和临床评分，实现阿尔茨海默病进展的连续评估。

Abstract: Alzheimer's disease (AD) progresses as a continuous biological process, whereas most existing neuroimaging-based artificial intelligence methods remain limited to discrete diagnosis or clinical score prediction from cross-sectional imaging. In this work, we propose Disease Continuum Positioning (DCP), a longitudinal Bayesian Learning framework that continuously estimates disease severity from longitudinal diffusion tensor imaging (DTI). Specifically, DCP models disease severity as a low-dimensional probabilistic latent variable by jointly integrating longitudinal observations with weak clinical supervision, from which the proposed Disease Continuum Score (DCS) is derived to quantify an individual's position along the Alzheimer's disease continuum together with its associated uncertainty. Extensive experiments on the Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative (ADNI) cohort demonstrate that DCP consistently outperforms representative disease progression methods. More importantly, comprehensive validation analyses show that DCS accurately characterizes disease severity, exhibits strong clinical relevance, preserves longitudinal disease evolution, and predicts future disease conversion. These results suggest that DCS provides a quantitative imaging-derived representation for continuous assessment of Alzheimer's disease progression beyond conventional diagnostic labels and clinical scores.

</details>


### [128] [Exact Algebraic Computation of Learning Coefficients for Two-Dimensional Singular Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20183)
*Grégoire Sergeant-Perthuis,Elias Tsigaridas,Jules Tsukahara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出首个确定性算法，可精确计算任意二维模型中与多项式接触等价的 Kullback-Leibler 距离的局部实对数典范阈值（RLCT，即学习系数 λ），并给出复杂度上界，在多项式神经网络等场景中验证了其有效性，且在浅层场景下优于采样法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典信息准则（如 BIC）依赖的正则性假设在奇异模型（如深度学习）中失效，导致模型选择错误；WBIC 依赖局部学习系数 λ 来刻画正确的边际似然渐近行为，但 λ 的精确计算此前仅限于特例，一般只能靠采样估计。缺乏精确、通用且高效的确定性计算手段。

Method: 设计首个确定性算法，对任意二维模型，当其 Kullback-Leibler 距离与某多项式接触等价时，精确计算局部 RLCT（局部学习系数 λ）；给出算法复杂度上界，并在多项式神经网络等一大类模型上进行实验验证。

Result: 算法能够精确算出二维模型的局部 RLCT，为采样估计器提供校准基准；揭示了采样无法发现的学习系数中的代数结构；在浅层（shallow）情形下比采样法更快。

Conclusion: 该确定性算法填补了奇异模型学习系数精确计算的空白，既可为采样法提供真值校准，又能揭示学习系数的代数结构，并在浅层模型中具有速度优势，为奇异模型选择理论和深度学习实践提供了新工具。

Abstract: Classical information criteria such as the Bayesian Information Criterion (BIC) rely on regularity assumptions that break down for singular models, leading to incorrect model selection in settings such as deep learning. The Widely Applicable Bayesian Information Criterion (WBIC) relies on local learning coefficients $λ$, which in the analytic case coincides with local Real Log Canonical Thresholds (RLCT) of the Kullback-Leibler divergence of the model, to capture correct marginal likelihood asymptotics. Exact computation of the learning coefficients has been limited to special cases, and only sampling-based estimation methods are generally applicable. We present the first deterministic algorithm that computes local RLCTs exactly for any two-dimensional model whose Kullback-Leibler distance is contact equivalent to a polynomial, derive a bound on its complexity, and demonstrate its effectiveness for a broad class of models, with applications including polynomial neural networks. Beyond providing ground truth to calibrate sampling-based estimators, exact computation reveals algebraic structure in learning coefficients that sampling cannot and out-speeds it in the shallow regime.

</details>


### [129] [Quantifying Event Impacts on Time Series via Multiscale Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19447)
*Yiming Sun,Shengyu Chen,Zhengzhang Chen,Haoyu Wang,Xiaowei Jia,Haifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EventTime 是一个多分辨率框架，结合长期市场背景、短期事件前动态与事件元数据，并通过动态对比学习缓解稀疏监督问题，用于预测网络安全披露后短期的异常金融损失。在真实数据集 SECURE 上，该方法显著优于现有时间序列和事件感知基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络安全漏洞披露等冲击性事件会突然扰乱金融时间序列并造成异常损失，但这些事件以离散记录披露，其后果却通过连续市场动态展开。现有时间序列预测模型主要关注趋势、季节性和自相关等内生规律，难以处理罕见且异质的的外部事件；同时高影响事件的稀疏性和市场背景噪声进一步放大了挑战。

Method: 提出 EventTime 多分辨率框架，融合长期市场背景、短期事件前动态和事件元数据；设计事件融合模块，将时序表示与事件属性耦合以识别相关近期市场模式；引入动态对比目标，在训练中构建事件感知和时间序列感知的正负样本对以缓解稀疏监督；构建真实数据集 SECURE，将网络安全事件与股市时间序列对齐，并提取结构化及 LLM 衍生的语义特征。

Result: 实验表明 EventTime 在估计事件后金融损失方面持续优于最先进的时间序列和事件感知基线；进一步分析显示其表示对事件更敏感、对元数据缺失更具鲁棒性，并能对网络安全披露后的短期市场影响给出更具可解释性的估计。

Conclusion: EventTime 有效解决了事件条件下的影响预测问题，能够从稀疏、异质的外部事件中估计短期异常损失，并在准确性、鲁棒性和可解释性上均表现优越，为金融领域的外部事件冲击建模提供了新思路。

Abstract: Shocks that spread through the web, such as cybersecurity breach disclosures, can abruptly disrupt financial time series and cause substantial abnormal losses. While these events are disclosed as discrete records through news reports, regulatory filings, or public databases, their consequences unfold through continuous market dynamics. This creates an event-conditioned impact prediction problem: given pre-event market history and limited event metadata, the goal is to estimate short-term post-disclosure abnormal loss rather than reconstruct the full post-event trajectory. However, most time-series forecasting models focus on endogenous regularities such as trend, seasonality, and autocorrelation, and thus struggle with rare and heterogeneous external events. The challenge is further amplified by sparse high-impact events and background market noise. We introduce EventTime, a multi-resolution framework that combines long-horizon market context, short-horizon pre-event dynamics, and event metadata. It incorporates an event fusion module that couples temporal representations with event attributes to identify relevant recent market patterns. To mitigate sparse supervision, EventTime further introduces a dynamic contrastive objective that constructs event- and time-series-aware positive and negative pairs during training. We also construct SECURE, a real-world dataset aligning cybersecurity incidents with stock-market time series and structured and LLM-derived semantic features. Experiments show that EventTime consistently outperforms state-of-the-art time-series and event-aware baselines in estimating post-event financial losses. Further analyses demonstrate more event-sensitive representations, greater robustness to incomplete metadata, and more interpretable estimates of short-term market impact following cybersecurity disclosures.

</details>


### [130] [DICS: Data-Informed Centroid Splitting for Decision Tree Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20258)
*MD Saifur Rahman Mazumder,Feng Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于聚类的分类树分裂点选择框架 DICS，用数据驱动的先验构造紧凑的候选分裂集，在保持预测精度的同时大幅缩短训练时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 决策树及其集成模型训练成本高，主要瓶颈在于每个节点对候选分裂点进行穷举搜索；对于大规模、高维数据集这一问题尤为突出，因此需要在不牺牲性能的前提下减少分裂搜索空间。

Method: 提出 Data-Informed Centroid Splitting (DICS)，一种基于聚类的框架：利用数据驱动的先验（并融入类别感知的类结构信息）构造一个紧凑且信息丰富的候选分裂集，从而显著缩小分类任务中的分裂搜索空间；该方法可嵌入分类树、随机森林和梯度提升模型。

Result: 理论分析表明，在给定假设下，DICS 相比穷举分裂搜索不会降低分类树的性能；在合成数据集与基准数据集上的大量实验显示，DICS 在取得可比准确率的同时大幅度减少了训练时间。

Conclusion: 将数据驱动的先验融入分裂选择是提升分类树学习可扩展性的有效途径，DICS 实现了效率与精度的良好平衡，适用于多种树基集成模型。

Abstract: Decision tree-based models are widely used in machine learning due to their interpretability and strong empirical performance. However, training decision trees can be computationally expensive, particularly for large and high-dimensional datasets, largely due to the exhaustive search over candidate splits at each node. To improve computational efficiency, we propose Data-Informed Centroid Splitting (DICS), a clustering-based framework that constructs a compact and informative set of candidate splits using data-driven priors. By incorporating class-aware structure, DICS significantly reduces the split search space for classification tasks while preserving predictive performance. We further provide theoretical analysis showing that under the stated assumptions, DICS does not degrade the performance of classification trees compared to exhaustive split search. DICS can be incorporated into classification trees, random forests, and gradient-boosting models. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DICS achieves comparable accuracy while substantially reducing training time across synthetic and benchmark datasets, highlighting the benefit of integrating data-informed priors into split selection for scalable classification tree learning.

</details>


### [131] [LLM as Detector: An In-context Learning Approach for Tabular Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19463)
*Tu Anh Hoang Nguyen,Dang Nguyen,Thuc Duy Le,Trung Le,Sunil Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 LLM-Detector 框架，利用大语言模型的上下文学习能力，将正常数据的统计摘要、因果依赖与原型转化为提示词，引导 LLM 生成无训练、无微调的异常评分引擎，在 24 个表格数据集上相较 15 个 SOTA 基线取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格数据的异常样本常表现为跨特征依赖的违反，而非简单的边缘分布偏差；现有检测器依赖几何或重构信号，而此前的 LLM 方法要么用正常样本微调、要么生成合成异常，均存在局限。为此需要一种能从结构化正常状态知识中直接推导异常检测逻辑的方法。

Method: 提出 LLM-Detector 框架，利用 LLM 的上下文学习能力进行结构化、提示词条件化的评分合成。具体做法是：将正常训练数据转换为统计摘要、因果依赖和提炼后的原型，组织成代码生成提示；由 LLM 生成的评分引擎对统计偏差、结构不一致性和基于密度的异常性进行评估，并为每个测试样本计算异常分数。

Result: 在 24 个表格数据集上与 15 个 SOTA 基线对比，LLM-Detector 在混合类型和仅连续型两种设置下均取得一致改进。

Conclusion: 该设计无需 LLM 微调或神经网络训练，降低了计算成本，使 LLM-Detector 能够实现实际表格系统中的实用异常检测，并在多种数据设置下稳定优于现有方法。

Abstract: Anomaly detection in tabular data is challenging because abnormal samples often arise as violations of cross-feature dependencies rather than simple marginal deviations. Existing detectors rely on geometric or reconstruction signals, while prior LLM-based approaches mainly fine-tune LLMs with normal samples or generate synthetic anomalies. We propose LLM-Detector, a framework that utilizes the in-context learning capacity of LLMs for structured, prompt-conditioned scoring synthesis, enabling LLMs to derive anomaly detection logic from structured normal-state knowledge. Specifically, normal training data are converted into statistical summaries, causal dependencies, and distilled prototypes that are organized into a prompt for code generation. The resulting scoring engine evaluates statistical deviation, structural inconsistency, and density-based abnormality then computes an anomaly score for each test sample. We evaluate LLM-Detector on 24 tabular datasets, comparing against 15 SOTA baselines. Results show consistent improvements across both mixed-type and continuous-only settings. Moreover, this design eliminates the need for LLM fine-tuning or neural network training, reducing computational cost and enabling practical anomaly detection in real-world tabular systems.

</details>


### [132] [When to Retrain: An Empirical Study of Retraining Policies for Streaming ML Under Concept Drift, Budget, and Latency Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19488)
*Sawan Dasari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过大规模受控实验研究了生产机器学习系统在概念漂移下的模型刷新策略，发现最关键的设计决策是模型是否增量学习，而非刷新策略本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产ML系统会因概念漂移而性能退化，但从业者缺乏何时重训的原则性指导；重训成本高、预算有限，且训练与部署的延迟使得新模型生效前数据仍在漂移，需要系统性地评估不同刷新策略的实际效果。

Method: 在统一系统模型（显式建模重训预算与训练+部署延迟）下，对三种刷新策略（周期重训、误差阈值触发、基于ADWIN的统计漂移触发重训）与不重训基线进行受控实证对比；共3933次实验运行，覆盖三种漂移模式、三种预算水平、最多五种延迟水平、三个数据集和两种学习模式。

Result: 在增量学习（逐样本更新、线性在线学习者、即时标签）下，54组配对比较中任何策略与不重训基线的差异均无实际显著意义，即使延迟极高；不采用增量更新时，策略选择使漂移后准确率相差15-55个百分点，周期重训在突发和渐进漂移下显著优于两种响应式策略，响应式策略仅在周期性漂移下占优；并记录了响应式策略的系统性失效模式，以及延迟-预算排队交互会悄然将有效重训预算减半。

Conclusion: 在有限预算与延迟下，是否让部署模型增量学习是比刷新策略选择影响更大的设计决策；周期重训在多数漂移场景更稳健，响应式策略存在明显失效模式。论文公开了完整模拟器、数据集流水线与逐次运行结果以供复现。

Abstract: Production machine learning systems degrade under concept drift, yet practitioners have little principled guidance on when to retrain. Retraining is costly, retraining budgets are finite, and a retrained model does not take effect instantly: training and deployment latency leave a stale model serving predictions while the data continues to move. We present a controlled empirical study of three practical model-refresh policies (periodic retraining, error-threshold triggering, and statistical drift-triggered retraining with ADWIN) against a no-retrain baseline, evaluated under a unified system model that makes retraining budgets and training-plus-deployment latency explicit. Across 3,933 experiment runs spanning three drift regimes, three budget levels, up to five latency levels, three datasets, and two learning modes, we find that the single most consequential design decision is not the retraining policy but whether the deployed model learns incrementally. With per-sample incremental updates, and for the linear online learner with immediate labels studied here, no policy differs from the no-retrain baseline by a practically significant margin in any of 54 paired comparisons, even at extreme latency. Without incremental updates, policy choice separates outcomes by 15-55 percentage points of post-drift accuracy, and simple periodic retraining significantly outperforms both reactive policies under abrupt and gradual drift, while reactive policies retain an advantage only under recurring drift. We document systematic failure modes of reactive policies and a latency-budget queueing interaction that silently halves effective retraining budgets, and release the full simulator, dataset pipelines, and per-run artifacts for reproducibility.

</details>


### [133] [Beyond Multimodal Alignment: Certifying Physical Language through Response Substitution and Ordered Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19492)
*Kaizhen Tan,Xin Xu,Siru Tao,Yixiao Li,Hanzhe Hong,Yang Feng,Heqing Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出操作能力层级与"分离桥接算子替换证书"(DBOSC),用于检验不同传感器模态的表征是否可互换地进入冻结响应图并在新动作组合下保持可执行意义。实验显示,在触觉簇上音频与加速度对同一未见面表征的响应空间距离比对错误表面配对近4.5倍(19个留出表面均成立);在具有互补盲区的弹塑性系统中,预注册预算下前置条件拒绝执行,而收敛预算下秩三图可执行程序(oracle NMSE 0.18),融合优于单模态,16项注册检验通过14项,失败源于对角限制融合信息矩阵与全矩阵表现相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型越来越多将紧凑多模态表征作为感知与物理交互之间的接口,但现有探针既未确立不同传感器是否携带相同的可执行意义,也未确立该意义在新动作组合下是否存活。因而需要一个操作化标准来分别检验属性获取、响应替换、融合闭合与有序执行。

Method: 作者构建操作能力层级,并引入分离桥接算子替换证书(DBOSC):询问独立训练的模态编译器能否在训练面板之外的证据上互换地进入冻结响应图。首先在Cluster Haptic数据集上比较音频与加速度表征在响应空间中的距离(同面 vs 错面配对的留出泛化实验);随后在受控弹塑性系统中测试有序执行,设置预注册预算检验前置条件是否拒绝栈,以及收敛预算下秩三图执行留出程序的性能,并与融合、实体盲预测器、发射整程序的执行器及非可辨识性分析对照。

Result: 在Cluster Haptic上,同一未见面表面的音频与加速度表征在响应空间中比错误表面配对近4.5倍,且这一差距对全部19个留出表面均成立;解封留出响应证实每个分支都比总体图更准确地预测物理。在弹塑性系统中:预注册预算下前置条件拒绝栈(冻结执行器无法推进坐标),收敛预算下同一秩三图执行程序(oracle NMSE 0.18);融合优于两种单模态;16项注册检验通过14项,两处失败源于对角限制的融合信息矩阵与全矩阵表现相当。清除门是执行器的属性而非图的属性:发射整程序的执行器比实体盲预测器差38倍。匹配的非可辨识性结果说明仅靠压缩与融合无法确定未见组合律。

Conclusion: 结果将属性获取、响应替换、融合闭合与有序执行分解为互不相同、可分别检验的成就。不同传感器可携带共享的可执行意义且能在新组合下存活,但这依赖于执行器结构而非表征图本身;压缩与融合单独不足以确定未见组合律。

Abstract: World models increasingly treat compact multimodal representations as interfaces between perception and physical interaction, yet existing probes do not establish whether different sensors carry the same executable meaning or whether that meaning survives a new action composition. We introduce an operational capability hierarchy and the Disjoint-Bridge Operator-Substitution Certificate (DBOSC), which asks whether independently trained modality compilers enter a frozen response chart interchangeably on evidence outside their training panels. On Cluster Haptic, audio and acceleration representations of the same unseen surface are 4.5x closer in response space than wrong-surface pairings, with the gap holding for all 19 held-out surfaces; unsealing withheld responses confirms that every branch predicts the physics better than the population chart. We then test ordered execution in a controlled elastoplastic system with complementary modality blind spots. At the pre-registered budget, the prerequisite refuses the stack because the frozen executor cannot advance even an exact chart coordinate through a held-out program. At a converged budget, the same rank-three chart executes those programs (oracle NMSE 0.18), fusion improves on both modalities, and 14 of 16 registered checks pass; the two failures arise because a diagonal restriction of the fused information matrix performs as well as the full one. Clearing the gate is a property of the executor, not the chart: an executor emitting whole programs instead of shared per-step dynamics is 38x worse than an entity-blind predictor on the same chart. A matching non-identifiability result explains why compression and fusion alone cannot determine an unseen composition law. These results separate attribute access, response substitution, fusion closure, and ordered execution into distinct, separately testable achievements.

</details>


### [134] [Empirical Characterization of Learning Geometry in Hybrid Quantum Forecasting Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19497)
*Sandra Leticia Juárez-Osorio,Jorge I. Hernandez-Martinez,Jesus Ivan Ruiz-Martinez,Andres Mendez-Vazquez,Eduardo Rodriguez-Tello*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过对比紧凑混合量子预测模型与结构对齐的经典基线，利用神经正切核（NTK）诊断工具（核-目标对齐、核漂移、谱集中度、训练损失）刻画二者学习动力学，发现尽管优化几何差异显著，两者泛化性能相近；混合模型参数更少且多数条件下更早收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对量子/混合模型的研究多仅关注端点准确率，掩盖了架构依赖的学习行为差异。作者旨在揭示混合量子模型与经典模型不同的优化轨迹和核几何，并检验单个NTK诊断指标能否作为验证收敛的单调预测器。

Method: 采用平稳谐波混合与非平稳啁啾基准，控制谱复杂度和数据可用性；对比紧凑混合量子模型与结构对齐的经典基线；通过经验NTK的核-目标对齐、核漂移、谱集中度和训练损失进行分析；并引入傅里叶增强经典基线与受控重上传消融实验。

Result: 经典模型早期目标对齐更强，混合模型核谱更不集中且核漂移更小；两者在各类条件下保持相近的留出集性能。混合模型仅用125个可训练参数（经典基线281个），且18个频率条件中有15个更早到达验证选定的检查点。傅里叶增强经典基线无法复现该训练行为，重上传消融表明重复编码系统性改变优化与核几何。

Conclusion: 相近的泛化能力可由显著不同的学习轨迹产生；单个NTK诊断指标并非验证收敛的单调预测器。研究并非主张普遍量子优势，而是识别出仅凭端点准确率无法发现的架构依赖学习行为。

Abstract: We characterize the learning dynamics of a compact hybrid quantum forecasting model through comparison with a structurally aligned classical baseline. Using stationary harmonic-mixture and nonstationary chirp benchmarks with controlled spectral complexity and data availability, we analyze empirical Neural Tangent Kernel dynamics through kernel-target alignment, kernel drift, spectral concentration, and training loss. The classical model exhibits stronger early target alignment, whereas the hybrid model generally develops a less concentrated kernel spectrum and smaller kernel drift. Despite these distinct optimization geometries, both architectures attain similar held-out performance across the evaluated regimes. Notably, the hybrid model uses 125 trainable parameters compared with 281 for the classical baseline and reaches its validation-selected checkpoint earlier in 15 of 18 frequency conditions. A Fourier-augmented classical baseline does not reproduce the observed training behavior, while a controlled re-uploading ablation shows that repeated encoding systematically modifies both optimization and kernel geometry. These results demonstrate that comparable generalization can emerge from substantially different learning trajectories and that individual NTK diagnostics do not provide monotonic predictors of validation convergence. Rather than claiming a general quantum advantage, the study identifies architecture-dependent learning behavior that is masked by endpoint accuracy alone.

</details>


### [135] [In Two Minds about Lifelong Learning: Exploring Hemispheric Redundancy and Specialisation in Neural Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19514)
*Benjamin Smith,Levin Kuhlmann,Kaushik Roy,Gideon Kowadlo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出4MAS（4模块觉醒/睡眠）宏观架构，利用双侧半球、经验回放与REM睡眠启发机制，在持续学习基准数据集上取得了有竞争力的结果，缓解灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续的智能系统需要持续学习能力，但现有机器学习算法在保留旧知识与适应新数据之间存在权衡，且在全量重训不可行（数据缺失、成本或隐私限制）时尤其困难；而生物动物能持续学习而不发生灾难性遗忘。本文旨在模仿生物的记忆巩固机制（经验回放、REM睡眠、双侧性）构建高级框架，使机器学习模型受益。

Method: 提出4MAS（4 Module Awake/Sleep）宏观架构，受生物学习启发，包含非对称（左右）半球、各自的长短期记忆机制，并在增量学习任务之间引入睡眠阶段以促进记忆巩固。该架构整合了经验回放、REM睡眠和双侧性三个核心概念。

Result: 在Split-MNIST、Split-Fashion-MNIST和Split-CIFAR-100三个持续学习基准上，4MAS分别取得98.3%、84.9%和29.29%的准确率，结果具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 4MAS展示了非对称半球结构与任务间睡眠期对记忆巩固的益处，为机器学习模型借鉴生物持续学习机制提供了有效的宏观架构方案，并取得了有竞争力的实验结果。

Abstract: Persistent intelligent systems require the ability to learn continually, but current machine learning approaches face significant challenges in this area compared to biological learning systems. Machine learning algorithms typically trade off retention of previously learned information and adaptation to new or changing data patterns. When continual learning capabilities are absent, algorithms must undergo retraining using the entire data set, an approach that becomes impractical when original training data are unavailable due to storage constraints, financial or computational costs, or privacy restrictions. However, biological animals can learn continually, without experiencing catastrophic forgetting. This paper attempts to build a high-level framework for how animals learn and preserve knowledge by modelling neural components and states that are known to be related to memory consolidation. We focus on three concepts: experience replay, REM sleep, and bilaterality. We propose 4MAS (4 Module Awake/Sleep), a novel macroarchitecture demonstrating how machine learning models might benefit from asymmetric hemispheres, each with their own long- and short-term memory mechanisms, and how a period of sleep between incremental learning tasks might benefit memory consolidation. Finally, we present results showing that our architecture achieves competitive results on the Split-MNIST, Split-Fashion-MNIST and Split-CIFAR-100 datasets, with 98.3%, 84.9%, and 29.29% accuracy respectively.

</details>


### [136] [Continuous Adversarial MeanFlow Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19540)
*Yara Bahram,Zahra Dehghani,Mélodie Desbos,Eric Granger,Pablo Piantanida,Mohammadhadi Shateri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 MeanFlow-Transfer（MF-T）与 Continuous Adversarial MeanFlow（CAMF），解决在有限数据下将预训练生成模型快速适配到新域并加速采样的难题：MF-T 将异构预训练模型的输出统一映射到共享的速度表征，以此初始化 MeanFlow 生成器并在目标域训练；CAMF 将连续对抗流模型从瞬时速度推广到 MF 的有限区间平均速度，恢复回归平均掉的细节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在有限数据下训练快速生成器面临两大挑战：其一，微调预训练扩散/流模型到新域并未解决其代价高昂的多步采样问题，且现有加速方法绑定于源参数化（ε、x、v、u），异构预训练模型缺乏统一的加速目标；其二，对抗细化虽已被证明对少步质量有效，但只适用于瞬时速度流，而 MeanFlow 模型预测的是有限区间平均速度，无法直接套用。
